# OctoSnap and the alternatives

OctoSnap set beside six other tools a GNOME user might reach for: **Gradia**, **Kooha**, **Flameshot**, **GNOME Shell's own screenshot tool**, **Shutter** and **ksnip**. Every mark on this page was read from each project's source code, at the commit named at the end, and links to the line it rests on: hover over a mark for what that tool does there, click it for the code. Nothing is taken from a README, a website or a changelog, and where a README promises something the code does not do, the code wins.

The comparison is for **GNOME on Wayland**, the desktop OctoSnap is made for. A feature a tool has only on X11, or only on Windows or macOS, is marked as such and not counted.

## In short

Of the 353 capabilities found in the seven tools' code, OctoSnap has **272** on GNOME Wayland. The next is ksnip with 168, then Flameshot with 149, Shutter with 146, Gradia with 138, GNOME Shell with 93 and Kooha with 88. 50 of them are in OctoSnap and in none of the others.

It is the only one of the seven with the whole path in one app: a capture overlay of its own on Wayland, a card for every capture, an annotation editor with redaction and backgrounds, scrolling capture, text and QR recognition, pins that stay above other windows, a history, and GIF recording with an editor for the result.

What it does not do yet is just as plain in the tables: it records GIFs but not video, and no sound; it uploads nowhere; it is in English only; and it runs on GNOME alone. [Where the others go further](#where-the-others-go-further) lists every such row.

✓ has it on GNOME Wayland · ◐ in part, hover for which part · ⊘ in the code, but not on GNOME Wayland · – not in the code, or not reachable. Each mark links to its code.

## Area by area

How many of each area's capabilities each tool has on GNOME Wayland, whole or in part.

| | Rows | OctoSnap | Gradia | Kooha | Flameshot | GNOME Shell | Shutter | ksnip |
|---|:-:|:-:|:-:|:-:|:-:|:-:|:-:|:-:|
| [Capturing](#capturing) | 28 | **20** | 13 | 12 | 13 | 13 | 9 | 11 |
| [Choosing the area](#choosing-the-area) | 26 | **20** | 1 | 10 | 15 | 11 | 1 | 2 |
| [After the capture](#after-the-capture) | 29 | **26** | 16 | 9 | 16 | 13 | 23 | 22 |
| [Annotation tools](#annotation-tools) | 37 | **28** | 16 | 0 | 21 | 1 | 19 | 26 |
| [The editor](#the-editor) | 54 | **48** | 33 | 1 | 21 | 2 | 37 | 39 |
| [Recording](#recording) | 32 | 20 | 0 | **25** | 0 | 16 | 0 | 0 |
| [Editing a recording](#editing-a-recording) | 10 | **10** | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| [Text recognition](#text-recognition) | 14 | **14** | 6 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 4 |
| [Pinned screenshots](#pinned-screenshots) | 15 | **14** | 7 | 0 | 10 | 0 | 0 | 9 |
| [History](#history) | 9 | **6** | 3 | 1 | 0 | 1 | **6** | 2 |
| [Sharing](#sharing) | 8 | 1 | 4 | 0 | 0 | 0 | **7** | 6 |
| [Scripting and automation](#scripting-and-automation) | 24 | **16** | 10 | 5 | 14 | 7 | 14 | 11 |
| [Shortcuts, menus and settings](#shortcuts-menus-and-settings) | 36 | **26** | 12 | 10 | 21 | 10 | 16 | 19 |
| [Platform](#platform) | 25 | **19** | 15 | 15 | 17 | 18 | 14 | 17 |
| [Extras](#extras) | 6 | **4** | 2 | 0 | 1 | 1 | 0 | 0 |
| **All** | **353** | **272** | 138 | 88 | 149 | 93 | 146 | 168 |

A row is one capability, so a big one and a small one count the same: these numbers say how much ground a tool covers, not how well it covers it. The tables below say how.

## Only in OctoSnap

50 capabilities that none of the other six has in its code, on any platform.

- **Capturing**: [Hide desktop icons during a capture or recording](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/extension/src/flow.ts#L191); [Scrolling capture (long screenshot)](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/extension/src/flow.ts#L294); [Scrolling capture sideways and upward](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/core/src/scroll.rs#L21); [Sticky headers and sidebars handled in scrolling capture](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/stitch/src/align.rs#L306) (in part).
- **Choosing the area**: [Reposition a selection while still drawing it](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/extension/src/overlay/engine.ts#L1056); [Aspect-ratio lock toggle and ratio presets](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/extension/src/overlay/toolbar.ts#L134); [On-screen controls for scrolling capture](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/extension/src/scroll.ts#L49); [Live preview of the stitched scrolling capture](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/scrolling/controls.rs#L59).
- **After the capture**: [Copy a recording to the clipboard](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/qao/card.rs#L1590); ['@2x' suffix for HiDPI captures](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/prefs.rs#L797).
- **Annotation tools**: [Curved arrow (bendable shaft)](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/scene/src/style.rs#L397); [Smart highlighter (snaps to the text line)](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/editor/options.rs#L840); [Counter numbering styles (letters, Roman numerals)](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/scene/src/style.rs#L558); [Spotlight (dim all but a region)](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/scene/src/tool.rs#L341).
- **The editor**: [Duplicate objects in one step](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/editor/window.rs#L771); [Editable project files](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/editor/actions.rs#L1373).
- **Recording**: [Recording quality setting](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/prefs.rs#L1203); [Output size cap (downscale)](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/media/src/gif.rs#L245); [Floating on-screen recording controls](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/recording/controls.rs#L30); [Lossless capture, deferred encoding](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/media/src/reel.rs#L238).
- **Editing a recording**: [Built-in editor for recordings](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/gif_editor.rs#L212); [Playback and frame-by-frame stepping](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/gif_editor.rs#L253); [Thumbnail timeline (filmstrip)](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/gif_editor.rs#L644); [Trim start and end](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/gif_editor.rs#L292); [Change frame rate of an existing recording](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/gif_editor.rs#L537); [Resize an existing recording](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/gif_editor.rs#L567); [Re-encode with a quality setting](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/gif_editor.rs#L589); [Export or keep the edited recording](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/gif_editor.rs#L466); [Edits rendered from lossless source frames](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/media/src/recorder.rs#L281); [Open an existing GIF in the recording editor](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/import.rs#L140).
- **Text recognition**: [Select an area and copy its text in one step](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/extension/src/overlay/toolbar.ts#L127); [Copy Text from a preview card](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/qao/card.rs#L1273); [Copy Text from a pinned screenshot](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/pin/window.rs#L933); [Automatic language/script detection](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/ocr/src/rapid/mod.rs#L127); [Automatic rescaling for small text](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/ocr/src/rapid/detect.rs#L91); [Paragraph-aware output with a keep/join line-breaks choice](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/ocr/src/shape.rs#L102); [QR code reading](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/ocr/src/qr.rs#L42); [Clickable links and e-mail addresses in recognised text](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/ocr/src/links.rs#L49).
- **Pinned screenshots**: [Lock a pin (click-through)](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/pin/window.rs#L670); [Pin updates when its capture is edited](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/editor/crop.rs#L636) (in part).
- **History**: [Filter the history by capture kind](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/history/strip.rs#L598); [Restore the last closed capture](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/qao/mod.rs#L586); [History retention and size limit](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/prefs.rs#L2297).
- **Scripting and automation**: [Screen recording from the command line](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/cli/src/main.rs#L172).
- **Shortcuts, menus and settings**: [Choice of shutter sound](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/prefs.rs#L408); [Sound cues for other actions](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/crates/app/src/prefs.rs#L530); [Hide or show desktop icons on demand](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/extension/src/indicator.ts#L277).
- **Extras**: [Desktop pets](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/extension/src/pets/art/index.ts#L24); [Start a capture from a desktop pet](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/extension/src/pets/crew.ts#L647); [Desktop pets never appear in captures or recordings](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/blob/v0.1.7/extension/src/pets/crew.ts#L253).

## Where the others go further

72 capabilities that at least one other tool has on GNOME Wayland and OctoSnap does not, or only in part.

- **Capturing**: Instant capture of the focused window: GNOME Shell, Shutter; Window chosen from a list or by title: Kooha; Capture launcher dialog: Kooha, Flameshot, Shutter, ksnip; Retake a chosen earlier capture: Shutter.
- **Choosing the area**: Custom overlay colours and cursor: Flameshot; Click places a default-size selection: Kooha, GNOME Shell; Annotate inside the capture overlay: Flameshot; Select the area in a window over a live preview: Kooha.
- **After the capture**: Export to PDF / PostScript: Shutter; Rename the saved capture: Shutter, ksnip; Date-based save subfolders: ksnip.
- **Annotation tools**: Text callout with pointer or arrow: ksnip; Automatic renumbering of step badges: Gradia, Flameshot, ksnip; Numbered badge with pointer or arrow: Flameshot, ksnip; Freehand redaction brush: Shutter; Invert colours in a region: Flameshot; Stickers / clip-art library: Shutter, ksnip; Custom sticker library: ksnip; Duplicate a picture region (clone stamp): ksnip.
- **The editor**: Object list (layers panel): Flameshot; Vector / PDF export: Shutter; Cut out a slice: ksnip; Grid, rulers and snap-to-grid: Flameshot; Image effects and filters: Flameshot, Shutter, ksnip; Watermark: ksnip.
- **Recording**: Record a single window: Kooha; Several monitors or windows in one recording: Kooha, GNOME Shell; Video recording (MP4 / WebM / MKV): Kooha, GNOME Shell; Choice of output format: Kooha; VP9 / AV1 codecs: Kooha; Hardware-accelerated encoding: Kooha, GNOME Shell; Record system (desktop) audio: Kooha; Record the microphone: Kooha; System audio and microphone together: Kooha; Rotated screens recorded upright: Kooha, GNOME Shell; Prevent screen blanking and logout while recording: Kooha.
- **Pinned screenshots**: Rotate a pin: Flameshot.
- **History**: Upload history: Shutter, ksnip; Recent files list: Gradia, Kooha, GNOME Shell, Shutter, ksnip; Open captures follow changes to their files: Shutter.
- **Sharing**: Upload to a public image host: Gradia, Shutter, ksnip; Upload to a signed-in account: Shutter, ksnip; FTP upload: Shutter, ksnip; Custom upload command, script or plugin: Gradia, Shutter, ksnip; Share link: copy, open, delete: Gradia, Shutter, ksnip; Send by e-mail or to another app: Shutter; Confirmation before upload: Gradia, ksnip.
- **Scripting and automation**: Write the image to standard output: Flameshot; Read an image from standard input: Gradia, ksnip; Man page and shell completions: Flameshot, Shutter; Change settings with the app's own command: Flameshot; Desktop launcher quick actions: Gradia, Flameshot, Shutter, ksnip; Named settings profiles: Shutter; Plugin system: Shutter, ksnip; User-defined capture actions: ksnip.
- **Shortcuts, menus and settings**: Start from GNOME's system menu or overview search: GNOME Shell; Main application window: Gradia, Kooha, Flameshot, Shutter, ksnip; Import, export or reset settings: Flameshot, Shutter; Built-in update check: Flameshot; Custom interface colours: Flameshot, Shutter, ksnip; Tool behaviour after drawing: ksnip; Status bar with pointer position and tool hints: Flameshot, Shutter; Protect a running recording: Kooha; In-app guide to bind a capture shortcut: Gradia; Visual flash on capture: GNOME Shell.
- **Platform**: Other Linux desktops (KDE Plasma, wlroots, ...): Gradia, Kooha, Flameshot, Shutter, ksnip; Windows and macOS builds: Flameshot, ksnip; UI translations: Gradia, Kooha, Flameshot, GNOME Shell, Shutter, ksnip; Upload credential storage: Shutter, ksnip; Screenshot provider for sandboxed apps: GNOME Shell.
- **Extras**: Code snippet images: Gradia; Bundled handwriting font for text: Gradia.

## How this was made

Each project was cloned from GitHub at the commit below, its default branch on 2026-10-07, and read, not run. The capabilities were first listed from each tool's code and gathered into the rows above, and one critic for each tool then looked for anything it does that no row covered. Every tool's column was filled from its code by one reader, row by row, with the lines that implement a capability or the searches that found none. A second reader then went over the cells the comparison turns on without taking the first one's word for them: every cell of OctoSnap's, and every cell where only one of OctoSnap and the other tool has the feature, trying hardest to find what the first had called missing. Where the two disagreed, a third read the code again and decided. Of the 2,471 cells, 1,679 were read twice and 21 were decided that way; the rest are cells where OctoSnap and the other tool agree.

OctoSnap was read at v0.1.4. Its column has been carried to each release since through the diff, line by line, and the cells each release touched were read again at it, twice, in the same way: 16 at v0.1.5, 17 at v0.1.6 and 17 at v0.1.7.

The readers were Claude agents, the same AI that writes OctoSnap, so the rule throughout was code over claims, in both directions: a tool is not credited for what its README says, and not denied what its code does.

| Tool | Read at | Language | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| OctoSnap | 0.1.7 | Rust and TypeScript | [odrakirmusic/OctoSnap](https://github.com/odrakirmusic/OctoSnap/tree/v0.1.7) @ `v0.1.7` |
| Gradia | main after 1.13.0 | Python | [AlexanderVanhee/Gradia](https://github.com/AlexanderVanhee/Gradia/tree/3ac3d8170233c835ea6b69f608a3d773c90950bb) @ `3ac3d81` |
| Kooha | main after 2.3.2 | Rust | [SeaDve/Kooha](https://github.com/SeaDve/Kooha/tree/247bd25579538035d9a87bfdf582258767d673c5) @ `247bd25` |
| Flameshot | master after 14.0.0 | C++ and Qt | [flameshot-org/flameshot](https://github.com/flameshot-org/flameshot/tree/3455978ec0b72779e5a1ff328f904a1250d58dc0) @ `3455978` |
| GNOME Shell | main, as released in 51.0 | JavaScript and C | [GNOME/gnome-shell](https://github.com/GNOME/gnome-shell/tree/29380c2662daaed660cd6d0115cdfe370510d8f6) @ `29380c2`, [GNOME/mutter](https://github.com/GNOME/mutter/tree/d82671c3035bfdb10fdc1ffd2c0e31859bb00ff7) @ `d82671c` |
| Shutter | master after 0.99.7 | Perl | [shutter-project/shutter](https://github.com/shutter-project/shutter/tree/cb78390f546e7ad93690ab64a8afb72a66e6128c) @ `cb78390` |
| ksnip | master after 1.10.1 | C++ and Qt | [ksnip/ksnip](https://github.com/ksnip/ksnip/tree/df69d12a08f0b941d70d3638721bbc7d7464f76a) @ `df69d12`, [ksnip/kColorPicker](https://github.com/ksnip/kColorPicker/tree/2781a262b6ae76ec2e9a2d86b3ab8892a90f06aa) @ `2781a26`, [ksnip/kImageAnnotator](https://github.com/ksnip/kImageAnnotator/tree/d184dbd77da385a7e2e9c7527f3a4184b0842cb4) @ `d184dbd` |

GNOME Shell stands for its screenshot UI and screen recorder only, with Mutter beside it for what the compositor does. ksnip's editor is its library kImageAnnotator, read with it. On GNOME Wayland, Gradia, Flameshot, Shutter and ksnip take their pictures through the desktop portal, and Kooha its recordings, so where GNOME's own chooser does the choosing for them, the tables say so.

Reading code is not running it. A mark says what the code does, as far as careful reading shows; it says nothing of how fast, how polished or how reliable a feature is. If a mark is wrong, an issue with the line of code that shows it is welcome, and the page will be corrected.
